ParlaMint-PL_2018-06-06-sejm-63-2.u416	Mr. Marshal, High Izbo! I have the honour on behalf of the Club of the Polish People's Party - the Union of European Democrats to present our position on the Senate bill amending the Local referendum law and the National referendum law (print No 2434). Today's proposal aims to align the right with the judgment of the Constitutional Tribunal of 20 April 2017 declaring the provisions of national referendum laws incompatible with the Constitution and the local referendum. The proposal introduces the possibility of reopening proceedings following the decision of the Court of Justice in matters relating to the dissemination of false information in statements, leaflets, etc. during the referendum campaign. In the case of the Local referendum law: If the posters, passwords, leaflets, statements or other forms of propaganda and agitation contain false data and information, the district court may be asked for: the confiscation of such materials; the issue of a ban on their publication; the order for correction; the order for an apology to be spoken; the order for the participant to proceed with payment of up to 10 000. PLN for a charity; convictions from a participant in proceedings to the applicant of the same amount as compensation. At present, the rules provide that the district court shall recognise these applications within 24 hours in non-trial proceedings. An appeal may be lodged, including within 24 hours, with the order of the Court of Appeal (II instance), after which a request for reopening of proceedings may not be submitted. This last provision challenged the Constitutional Court by judgment of 20 April 2017. The Court pointed out that in the so-called case law. referenda proceedings are very short time limits for the examination of the case at first and second instance to ensure that the decision is issued in such a time that voters can, on the one hand, read the court's findings before the day of the vote and, on the other hand, that a fair pre-election debate, free from pathology related to the dissemination of false messages, takes place for a sufficiently long period of time to form the will of voters. However, according to the TK, the simplification and shortening of the procedure must not result in a finding which is incorrect, i.e. not in line with the requirements of the rule of law. He pointed out that it should be possible to touch the final judgment of the court, which is seriously flawed. This applies in particular to a situation in which new evidence emerges as soon as the judgment is delivered, which shows that the final judgment does not correspond to the reality - underlined in the explanatory memorandum. TK also noted that in the case of electoral proceedings, due to their speed, the risk of misconceptions is significantly increased. High Izbo! Undoubtedly, in the case of decisions in a special electoral procedure, the speed of proceedings is crucial. What will happen to the victim from a favourable judgment when he is voted out, in this case, after the referendum? However, the speed and simplification of the procedure must not lead to a limitation of the right to raise a judgment in the event of serious (qualified) defects. The current exclusion of the possibility to bring forward judgments in the present case has infringed the constitutional right to consider the matter in fair proceedings.

It has high, noble cereals to enrich Polish tables. Great spirit and deed grew on it. Great people aren't born on the verge of death. They were shaped by a culture rich in values that cannot pass away. They were born and established by the atmosphere of the noble houses. These houses, contrary to communist messages, are the birthplaces of Polish culture and elevated patriotism. In the noble houses, Dąbrowski's Mazurek resounded constantly because our anthem won the clocks. On the walls there were images with images of national heroes created on Polish saints, and when they could not hang, because it was forbidden by censorship, hunting trophies, dried flowers and herbs from Polish fields were hung. They symbolized the wealth of Polish forests and fields, the beauty of our nature and Polish soul. You can already see that the Polish house is unique and plays a huge role in the family. He went through various stages of development, but who among us doesn't know what the noble house, the manor house, looked like? From various cultural products we know that he was noble, ancient, God-fearing, hospitable, safe, cheerful, with characteristic surroundings: trees in the yard, guesthouse and entrance gate hospitably opened. This house is as real as it is dreamy, unique. He himself gained great value, to which he had already been appealed at every moment of his life, especially in moments of misfortune, separation. He slowly became a symbol of the whole country. Our family law was completely different from that in Europe. That's where the eldest son inherited his estate. The rest of the siblings were equipped for life and the family home was leaving. With us, the property belonged to the whole family and all the children received their share. Although this was the cause of the property's fragmentation, family ties remained closer, without any sense of harm. Poles chose a sense of family and justice. The family home did not lose its warmth, atmosphere of proximity, communality. I think that's why there were values born in this house that our nation stood out. They became a Polish cultural centre because they included central values, the most important ones. They always included: family land, national colors - white and red - emblem, mother tongue, faith, respect for work, love of freedom, personal dignity, ability to make unlimited sacrifices and sacrifices in the name of community, honor and obedience, family and family, family home, hospitality, generosity, respect for women, love of children, attachment to their own history and belief in its uniqueness, as well as hope contrary to hope, lack of cruelty, forgiving ability, tolerance, citizenship, democracy and criticism of power. The patterns and values of the noble house moved to peasant huts and bourgeois families. Hence the durability of Polish culture. If someone forgets about it today, it surprises them: How do you, a Pole, not know it? Older people, hearing about someone's unworthy conduct, ask: How can he, being a Pole, do this? It is time to revive our national heroes, remind them of their greatness, their fortitude, and their sacrificial deeds. On us on the 100th anniversary of regaining independence, on all Poles it is the duty to teach how to love the country with its difficult freedom. Thank you. [[Applause]]
